<div class="Modal-dialog">
<p class="Modal-title">Make FireRescue1 your homepage <a class="Modal-titleIcon" data-dismiss="modal" href="#"></a></p>
<div class="Modal-body">
<ol class="List List--numbered u-textMedium">
<li class="List-item">Open the tools menu in your browser. This may be called “Tools” or use an icon like the cog  <i class="Icon Icon--cog u-textSizeM2 Icon--sparce u-textGray"></i> or menu bars <i class="Icon Icon--menu u-textSizeM2 Icon--sparce u-textGray"></i></li>
<li class="List-item">Select the option or tab named “Internet Options (Internet Explorer)”, “Options (Firefox)”, “Preferences (Safari)” or “Settings (Chrome)”.</li>
<li class="List-item">Look for a box or option labeled “Home Page (Internet Explorer, Firefox, Safari)” or “On Startup (Chrome)”.</li>
<li class="List-item">Enter “https://www.firerescue1.com/” and click OK.</li>
</ol>
<hr class="Modal-hr"/>
<p class="u-textSmall u-textGray">If you need further help setting your homepage, check your browser’s Help menu</p>

<span class="Page-content">
<h1 class="Page-metaItem">The One Resource for Firefighters</h1>

<div class="Page-sidebar">
